- **X9 QRCode - Senior Developer Skill**: Senior Developer Skill for the Go build on PostgreSQL: the repository's conventions, test-first, and the X9.150 domain decisions ([matera-inc/x9.150-qrcode](https://github.com/matera-inc/x9.150-qrcode/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/senior-developer) · ★2)
- **x9-2-0-14ev**: Cria apresentações estratégicas no padrão visual Genspark/V4, com saída em HTML de alta fidelidade e plano de conversão para Google Slides editável. Use quando o usuário pedir decks, apresentações, slides, Google Slides, PPTX, HTML deck, planejamento estratégico, semana 2/3/4, ou quiser transformar um arquivo consolidado de cliente em apresentação visual editável. ([edervasconcelos/skill-x9-apresentacoes-v4](https://github.com/edervasconcelos/skill-x9-apresentacoes-v4/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **x9-agent-instructions**: Write or review prompts, task briefs, and agent-instruction files — «напиши промпт», «проверь инструкции», "review this agent prompt". Do not use as the primary workflow for repository onboarding files, skill authoring, ordinary prose, or executing or delegating the described task. ([xonika9/agent-skills](https://github.com/xonika9/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/x9-agent-instructions) · ★5)
- **x9-appsec**: Secure development and source-based review for public websites and web-facing services, including API-only backends, workers, data stores, and deployment. Use for security-sensitive changes and pre-release audits; active testing of running targets requires separate authorization and scope. ([xonika9/agent-skills](https://github.com/xonika9/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/x9-appsec) · ★5)
- **x9-architecture-scout**: Use when the user asks for a read-only architecture audit that compares plausible directions and makes component boundaries, dependencies, ownership, coupling, or change hotspots visible — «проведи архитектурный аудит», «покажи проблемные места архитектуры», "audit this codebase architecture". Do not use to implement a refactor, create a general implementation plan, or merely explain one file. ([xonika9/agent-skills](https://github.com/xonika9/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/x9-architecture-scout) · ★5)
- **x9-better-questions**: Use whenever the user shares notes or a transcript of a talk with a customer, user, or potential client, drafts questions for such talks, starts or sums up a series of customer interviews (custdev), or wants to rehearse one with you playing the customer — the method of Rob Fitzpatrick's The Mom Test («Спроси маму») for getting facts instead of polite approval, for example «разбери разговор с клиентом», «вот заметки после встречи», «проверь мои вопросы», «начинаю серию интервью», «потренируй меня перед кастдевом», "prepare customer interview questions". Do not use for surveys or questionnaires… ([xonika9/agent-skills](https://github.com/xonika9/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/x9-better-questions) · ★5)
- **x9-browser-session**: Use when browser work requires choosing between a connector, a local browser, and an existing authenticated session, or when an agent must safely operate the user's logged-in browser - «открой в моём браузере», «используй мою сессию», «поработай в залогиненном браузере», "use my logged-in browser". Do not use for ordinary web research that can be completed with direct HTTP/search tools or for browser implementation details already owned by a project-specific test skill. ([xonika9/agent-skills](https://github.com/xonika9/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/x9-browser-session) · ★5)
- **x9-codex-delegation**: Claude Code only; never use in a Codex session. Use when substantial, well-scoped work should actually be delegated to Codex — «отдай в Codex», «делегируй Codex», «передай эту часть работы в Codex, чтобы распределить лимиты», "use Codex", implementation, migration, codebase analysis, logged-in browser work, UI verification, or a second-opinion review. Do not use for checking or planning subscription quotas without a concrete task, trivial work, choosing between Claude models, merely drafting a prompt/brief that is not explicitly being sent to Codex, or generating images (x9-codex-imagegen). ([xonika9/agent-skills](https://github.com/xonika9/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/x9-codex-delegation) · ★5)
- **x9-codex-imagegen**: Use when an agent outside Codex, such as Claude Code or OpenCode on any model, should generate or edit a raster image through the user's Codex subscription — «сгенерируй картинку через Codex», «нарисуй иллюстрацию», "generate an image with Codex". Do not use inside a Codex session, where Codex's own imagegen skill applies, or for editable diagrams, crops, resizes, or other edits made locally without generation, or delegating non-image work to Codex. ([xonika9/agent-skills](https://github.com/xonika9/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/x9-codex-imagegen) · ★5)
